Scenario overview
    The NIST Secure Software Development Framework (SSDF) provides a comprehensive set of practices for developing secure software throughout the software development lifecycle. Organizations subject to regulatory requirements, government contracts, or those seeking to implement industry-leading security practices often need to demonstrate compliance with the SSDF.
This guide provides actionable recommendations for how GitHub Enterprise customers can leverage GitHub features and integrations to meet SSDF requirements. class="hx-whitespace-nowrap hx-transition-colors hx-font-medium hx-text-gray-700 contrast-more:hx-font-bold contrast-more:hx-text-current dark:hx-text-gray-100 contrast-more:dark:hx-text-current">Implementing the NIST SSDF with GitHub</div></div><div class=content><h1>Implementing the NIST SSDF with GitHub</h1><div class="hx-flex hx-flex-col hx-subpixel-antialiased hx-gap-1"><div class="hx-mt-2 hx-text-sm hx-text-gray-700 dark:hx-text-gray-300 ltr:hx-text-left rtl:hx-text-right hx-flex hx-gap-1 hx-font-sans hx-font-semibold hx-tracking-tight not-prose"><span>Greg Mohler</span><span>&#183;</span><a href=https://github.com/CallMeGreg>@CallMeGreg</a></div><div class="hx-text-sm hx-text-gray-500 ltr:hx-text-left rtl:hx-text-right dark:hx-text-gray-400 hx-flex hx-gap-4 hx-font-mono hx-tracking-tight hx-whitespace-nowrap"><div>January 20, 2026</div><div>|</div><div>Updated <time datetime=2026-01-23T12:26:27.000Z>January 23, 2026</time></div></div></div><h2>Scenario overview<span class="hx-absolute -hx-mt-20" id=scenario-overview></span>
<a href=#scenario-overview class=subheading-anchor aria-label="Permalink for this section"></a></h2><p>The <a href=https://csrc.nist.gov/pubs/sp/800/218/final target=_blank rel=noopener>NIST Secure Software Development Framework</a> (SSDF) provides a comprehensive set of practices for developing secure software throughout the software development lifecycle. Organizations subject to regulatory requirements, government contracts, or those seeking to implement industry-leading security practices often need to demonstrate compliance with the SSDF.</p><p>This guide provides actionable recommendations for how GitHub Enterprise customers can leverage GitHub features and integrations to meet SSDF requirements. Each practice is categorized as:</p><ul><li><strong>GitHub-native</strong>: Can be implemented using built-in GitHub features</li><li><strong>GitHub Integration</strong>: Requires integration with external tools or services</li><li><strong>Out of scope</strong>: Relates to organizational processes or policies outside of GitHub&rsquo;s technical capabilities</li></ul><p>For organizations implementing the SSDF, GitHub provides a strong foundation across multiple practice areas including source code management, secure CI/CD pipelines, vulnerability detection, and supply chain security. This guide helps you understand which GitHub features to enable, how to configure them, and what additional integrations may be needed to achieve comprehensive SSDF compliance.</p><p>The full NIST SSDF v1.1 specification is available at: <a href=https://nvlpubs.nist.gov/nistpubs/SpecialPublications/NIST.SP.800-218.pdf>NIST SP 800-218</a></p><h2>Key design strategies and checklist<span class="hx-absolute -hx-mt-20" id=key-design-strategies-and-checklist></span>
<a href=#key-design-strategies-and-checklist class=subheading-anchor aria-label="Permalink for this section"></a></h2><h3>Design strategies<span class="hx-absolute -hx-mt-20" id=design-strategies></span>
<a href=#design-strategies class=subheading-anchor aria-label="Permalink for this section"></a></h3><p><strong>Leverage GitHub-native security features</strong>: GitHub provides extensive built-in capabilities for secure software development, including code scanning, secret scanning, Dependabot, and branch protection. Maximize use of these features before adding external integrations.</p><p><strong>Implement defense in depth</strong>: Use multiple layers of security controls including repository permissions, branch protection rules, required status checks, code review requirements, and security scanning. No single control should be the only barrier to introducing vulnerabilities.</p><p><strong>Automate security checks in CI/CD</strong>: Integrate security scanning, dependency checking, and policy enforcement directly into GitHub Actions workflows. Automated checks provide consistent enforcement and reduce human error.</p><p><strong>Establish clear governance boundaries</strong>: Use organization-level policies, repository rulesets, and custom properties to enforce security requirements consistently across repositories. Centralized governance reduces administrative overhead and ensures consistent application of security practices. For more detailed recommendations around repository rulesets and custom properties, see <a href=https://wellarchitected.github.com/library/governance/recommendations/managing-repositories-at-scale/ target=_blank rel=noopener>Managing Repositories at Scale</a>.</p><p><strong>Maintain comprehensive audit trails</strong>: Enable and preserve audit logs, workflow run histories, and security alert data. These artifacts provide evidence of security practices and support incident response and compliance reporting.</p><h3>Implementation checklist<span class="hx-absolute -hx-mt-20" id=implementation-checklist></span>
<a href=#implementation-checklist class=subheading-anchor aria-label="Permalink for this section"></a></h3><ul><li><input disabled type=checkbox> Configure organization-level security policies and settings</li><li><input disabled type=checkbox> Implement repository rulesets on all default branches</li><li><input disabled type=checkbox> Require code review approval before merging changes</li><li><input disabled type=checkbox> Enable secret scanning and push protection</li><li><input disabled type=checkbox> Enable Dependabot alerts and security updates</li><li><input disabled type=checkbox> Enable and configure code scanning to detect vulnerable coding patterns</li><li><input disabled type=checkbox> Perform dynamic/runtime security testing for executable code</li><li><input disabled type=checkbox> Use secure CI/CD build environments with least privilege permissions</li><li><input disabled type=checkbox> Configure required status checks for security scans in CI/CD</li><li><input disabled type=checkbox> Authenticate between CI/CD and cloud providers using OpenID Connect (OIDC)</li><li><input disabled type=checkbox> Implement role-based access control using teams and custom roles</li><li><input disabled type=checkbox> Enable SAML SSO or OIDC for user authentication to repositories</li><li><input disabled type=checkbox> Configure audit log streaming to a SIEM or log management system</li><li><input disabled type=checkbox> Establish processes for vulnerability triage and remediation</li><li><input disabled type=checkbox> Generate and maintain SBOMs for software releases</li><li><input disabled type=checkbox> Implement artifact attestations for build provenance</li><li><input disabled type=checkbox> Document security requirements in GitHub Issues or Projects</li><li><input disabled type=checkbox> Create security policies and incident response procedures</li><li><input disabled type=checkbox> Provide security training for developers and administrators</li><li><input disabled type=checkbox> Perform threat modeling and design reviews for critical applications</li></ul><h2>Assumptions and preconditions<span class="hx-absolute -hx-mt-20" id=assumptions-and-preconditions></span>
<a href=#assumptions-and-preconditions class=subheading-anchor aria-label="Permalink for this section"></a></h2><p>This implementation guide assumes:</p><ul><li><p><strong>GitHub Enterprise deployment</strong>: Your organization uses GitHub Enterprise Cloud (GHEC) or GitHub Enterprise Server (GHES).</p></li><li><p><strong>Administrative access</strong>: You have organization owner or administrator privileges to configure organization-level security settings, policies, and integrations.</p></li><li><p><strong>Advanced Security licensing</strong>: For private repositories, GitHub Advanced Security licenses are available to enable code scanning, secret scanning, and dependency review features.</p></li><li><p><strong>CI/CD pipeline adoption</strong>: Your organization uses or plans to use GitHub Actions or integrated CI/CD tools for build and deployment automation.</p></li><li><p><strong>Identity provider integration</strong>: Your organization has an identity provider (IdP) that can integrate with GitHub for SSO via SAML or OIDC.</p></li><li><p><strong>Security team involvement</strong>: Security professionals are engaged in defining requirements, reviewing architecture, and managing vulnerability response processes.</p></li><li><p><strong>Commitment to secure development</strong>: Leadership supports allocating time and resources to implement security practices throughout the SDLC.</p></li></ul><h2>Recommended deployment<span class="hx-absolute -hx-mt-20" id=recommended-deployment></span>
<a href=#recommended-deployment class=subheading-anchor aria-label="Permalink for this section"></a></h2><p>This section provides GitHub-specific implementation guidance for each NIST SSDF practice. Practices are organized by the framework&rsquo;s practice groups:</p><ul><li>Prepare the Organization (PO)</li><li>Protect the Software (PS)</li><li>Produce Well-Secured Software (PW)</li><li>Respond to Vulnerabilities (RV)</li></ul><h3>Prepare the Organization (PO)<span class="hx-absolute -hx-mt-20" id=prepare-the-organization-po></span>
<a href=#prepare-the-organization-po class=subheading-anchor aria-label="Permalink for this section"></a></h3><h4>PO.1: Define security requirements for software development<span class="hx-absolute -hx-mt-20" id=po1-define-security-requirements-for-software-development></span>
<a href=#po1-define-security-requirements-for-software-development class=subheading-anchor aria-label="Permalink for this section"></a></h4><p><strong>Summary</strong>: Establish and maintain security requirements for both development infrastructure and the software being developed.</p><p><strong>GitHub implementation scope</strong>: <em>GitHub-native</em></p><p><strong>Implementation details</strong>:</p><p>Use GitHub Issues and Projects to document, track, and maintain security requirements.</p><ol><li>Create a security requirements repository to centralize documentation</li><li>Use GitHub Issues to document individual security requirements with labels for categorization (e.g., <code>requirement:infrastructure</code>, <code>requirement:software</code>, <code>requirement:compliance</code>)</li><li>Create GitHub Projects boards to track requirement status and reviews</li><li>Use issue templates to standardize requirement documentation format</li><li>Enable Discussions for stakeholder input on evolving requirements</li><li>Link requirements to implementation work items for traceability</li></ol><div class="hx-overflow-x-auto hx-mt-6 hx-flex hx-rounded-lg hx-border hx-py-2 ltr:hx-pr-4 rtl:hx-pl-4 contrast-more:hx-border-current contrast-more:dark:hx-border-current hx-border-blue-200 hx-bg-blue-100 hx-text-blue-900 dark:hx-border-blue-200/30 dark:hx-bg-blue-900/30 dark:hx-text-blue-200"><div class="ltr:hx-pl-3 ltr:hx-pr-2 rtl:hx-pr-3 rtl:hx-pl-2"><div class="hx-select-none hx-text-xl" style="font-family:apple color emoji,segoe ui emoji,segoe ui symbol">ℹ️</div></div><div class="hx-w-full hx-min-w-0 hx-leading-7"><div class="hx-mt-6 hx-leading-7 first:hx-mt-0">Create a <a href=https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/issues/planning-and-tracking-with-projects/managing-your-project/managing-project-templates-in-your-organization target=_blank rel=noopener>template project board</a> for security requirements or use the <a href=https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/issues/planning-and-tracking-with-projects/creating-projects/copying-an-existing-project target=_blank rel=noopener>copy project</a> feature to quickly set up boards for new projects or teams.</div></div></div><p><strong>Configuration example</strong>:</p><div class="hextra-code-block hx-relative hx-mt-6 first:hx-mt-0 hx-group/code"><div><div class=highlight><pre tabindex=0 class=chroma><code class=language-yaml data-lang=yaml><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=c># .github/ISSUE_TEMPLATE/security-requirement.yml</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w></span><span class=nt>name</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>Security Requirement</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w></span><span class=nt>description</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>Document a security requirement for software development</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w></span><span class=nt>title</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=s2>&#34;[REQ]: &#34;</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w></span><span class=nt>labels</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=p>[</span><span class=s2>&#34;security-requirement&#34;</span><span class=p>]</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w></span><span class=nt>body</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>  </span>- <span class=nt>type</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>dropdown</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>    </span><span class=nt>id</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>category</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>    </span><span class=nt>attributes</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span><span class=nt>label</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>Requirement Category</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span><span class=nt>options</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span>- <span class=l>Infrastructure Security</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span>- <span class=l>Software Security</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span>- <span class=l>Process &amp; Policy</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span>- <span class=l>Compliance &amp; Regulatory</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>  </span>- <span class=nt>type</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>textarea</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>    </span><span class=nt>id</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>description</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>    </span><span class=nt>attributes</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span><span class=nt>label</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>Requirement Description</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span><span class=nt>description</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>Detailed description of the security requirement</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>    </span><span class=nt>validations</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span><span class=nt>required</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=kc>true</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>  </span>- <span class=nt>type</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>textarea</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>    </span><span class=nt>id</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>rationale</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>    </span><span class=nt>attributes</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span><span class=nt>label</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>Rationale</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span><span class=nt>description</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>Why this requirement is necessary</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>  </span>- <span class=nt>type</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>dropdown</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>    </span><span class=nt>id</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>priority</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>    </span><span class=nt>attributes</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span><span class=nt>label</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>Priority</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span><span class=nt>options</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span>- <span class=l>Critical</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span>- <span class=l>High</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span>- <span class=l>Medium</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span>- <span class=l>Low</span></span></span></code></pre></div></div><div class="hextra-code-copy-btn-container hx-opacity-0 hx-transition group-hover/code:hx-opacity-100 hx-flex hx-gap-1 hx-absolute hx-m-[11px] hx-right-0 hx-top-0"><button class="hextra-code-copy-btn hx-group/copybtn hx-transition-all active:hx-opacity-50 hx-bg-primary-700/5 hx-border hx-border-black/5 hx-text-gray-600 hover:hx-text-gray-900 hx-rounded-md hx-p-1.5 dark:hx-bg-primary-300/10 dark:hx-border-white/10 dark:hx-text-gray-400 dark:hover:hx-text-gray-50" title="Copy code"><div class="copy-icon group-[.copied]/copybtn:hx-hidden hx-pointer-events-none hx-h-4 hx-w-4"></div><div class="success-icon hx-hidden group-[.copied]/copybtn:hx-block hx-pointer-events-none hx-h-4 hx-w-4"></div></button></div></div><h4>PO.2: Implement roles and responsibilities<span class="hx-absolute -hx-mt-20" id=po2-implement-roles-and-responsibilities></span>
<a href=#po2-implement-roles-and-responsibilities class=subheading-anchor aria-label="Permalink for this section"></a></h4><p><strong>Summary</strong>: Define and assign security-related roles across the software development lifecycle, provide training, and obtain management commitment.</p><p><strong>GitHub implementation scope</strong>: <em>GitHub-native</em> for role definition and assignment; <em>Out of scope</em> for training programs and management commitment processes</p><p><strong>Implementation details</strong>:</p><p>Use GitHub&rsquo;s built-in role-based access control features.</p><ol><li><strong>Organization roles</strong>: Assign organization-level roles (Owner, Member) based on responsibilities, keeping the number of organization owners limited but more than one</li><li><strong>Team structure</strong>: Create teams that align with SDLC roles (e.g., <code>security-champions</code>, <code>code-reviewers</code>, <code>release-managers</code>)</li><li><strong>Repository roles</strong>: Use built-in repository roles (Admin, Maintain, Write, Triage, Read) or create custom repository roles</li><li><strong>CODEOWNERS file</strong>: Define code owners and required reviewers using a <a href=https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/repositories/managing-your-repositorys-settings-and-features/customizing-your-repository/about-code-owners target=_blank rel=noopener>CODEOWNERS</a> file</li><li><strong>Security managers role</strong>: Use the security manager role to grant security teams visibility across the organization</li></ol><div class="hx-overflow-x-auto hx-mt-6 hx-flex hx-rounded-lg hx-border hx-py-2 ltr:hx-pr-4 rtl:hx-pl-4 contrast-more:hx-border-current contrast-more:dark:hx-border-current hx-border-blue-200 hx-bg-blue-100 hx-text-blue-900 dark:hx-border-blue-200/30 dark:hx-bg-blue-900/30 dark:hx-text-blue-200"><div class="ltr:hx-pl-3 ltr:hx-pr-2 rtl:hx-pr-3 rtl:hx-pl-2"><div class="hx-select-none hx-text-xl" style="font-family:apple color emoji,segoe ui emoji,segoe ui symbol">ℹ️</div></div><div class="hx-w-full hx-min-w-0 hx-leading-7"><div class="hx-mt-6 hx-leading-7 first:hx-mt-0">Training programs and management commitment should be managed outside GitHub through your organization&rsquo;s learning management and communication systems.</div></div></div><h4>PO.3: Implement supporting toolchains<span class="hx-absolute -hx-mt-20" id=po3-implement-supporting-toolchains></span>
<a href=#po3-implement-supporting-toolchains class=subheading-anchor aria-label="Permalink for this section"></a></h4><p><strong>Summary</strong>: Use automation tools throughout the SDLC to improve security, and configure them to generate artifacts demonstrating security practices.</p><p><strong>GitHub implementation scope</strong>: <em>GitHub Integration</em></p><p><strong>Implementation details</strong>:</p><p>Combine GitHub-native security features with Actions-based automation.</p><p><strong>Enable organization-wide security features</strong>:</p><ol><li><p><strong>Security configurations</strong>: Use GitHub&rsquo;s <a href=https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/code-security/concepts/security-at-scale/about-security-configurations target=_blank rel=noopener>security configurations</a> feature to enable, enforce, and set as default the following security features across all repositories:</p><ul><li><strong>Secret scanning and push protection</strong>: Prevent credential exposure by detecting and blocking secrets before they are committed</li><li><strong>Code scanning</strong>: Automated SAST scanning using CodeQL to detect vulnerable coding patterns</li><li><strong>Dependabot alerts and security updates</strong>: Automatically detect and remediate vulnerable dependencies</li></ul><p>Security configurations provide centralized management and enforcement, reducing administrative overhead while ensuring comprehensive coverage across your repositories. For detailed guidance on creating and applying security configurations, refer to the <a href=https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/code-security/how-tos/secure-at-scale/configure-enterprise-security/establish-complete-coverage/creating-a-custom-security-configuration-for-your-enterprise target=_blank rel=noopener>GitHub documentation</a>.</p></li><li><p><strong>Immutable releases</strong>: Prevent modifications to published releases</p><ul><li>Enable immutable releases on repositories to ensure release artifacts cannot be altered after publication</li><li>This provides assurance that distributed software matches the original release</li></ul></li><li><p><strong>Artifact attestations</strong>: Sign artifacts during build and validate provenance before consumption</p><ul><li>Use GitHub&rsquo;s artifact attestation feature to cryptographically sign build artifacts</li><li>Enable consumers to verify artifact provenance and integrity using <code>gh attestation verify</code></li><li>Implements supply chain security best practices aligned with the <a href=https://slsa.dev/ target=_blank rel=noopener>SLSA framework</a></li></ul></li></ol><h4>PO.4: Define and use criteria for software security checks<span class="hx-absolute -hx-mt-20" id=po4-define-and-use-criteria-for-software-security-checks></span>
<a href=#po4-define-and-use-criteria-for-software-security-checks class=subheading-anchor aria-label="Permalink for this section"></a></h4><p><strong>Summary</strong>: Establish criteria for evaluating software security and track compliance throughout the SDLC.</p><p><strong>GitHub implementation scope</strong>: <em>GitHub-native</em></p><p><strong>Implementation details</strong>:</p><p>Use GitHub repository rulesets to define and enforce software security policies.</p><ol><li><strong>Repository rulesets</strong>: Create organization-level rulesets to monitor and enforce security requirements, monitoring any bypass activity</li><li><strong>Require code scanning results</strong>: In your rulesets, define the security alert severity that should block pull request merges</li><li><strong>Status checks</strong>: Use required status checks to enforce any additional criteria such as linters, unit tests, or custom security checks</li></ol><p>For more detailed examples of repository rulesets, see <a href=https://wellarchitected.github.com/library/governance/recommendations/managing-repositories-at-scale/rulesets-best-practices/ target=_blank rel=noopener>Rulesets Best Practices</a>.</p><h4>PO.5: Implement and maintain secure environments<span class="hx-absolute -hx-mt-20" id=po5-implement-and-maintain-secure-environments></span>
<a href=#po5-implement-and-maintain-secure-environments class=subheading-anchor aria-label="Permalink for this section"></a></h4><p><strong>Summary</strong>: Protect development, build, test, and distribution environments from compromise.</p><p><strong>GitHub implementation scope</strong>: <em>GitHub Integration</em></p><p><strong>Implementation details</strong>:</p><p>Use GitHub Actions environments and secure runner practices.</p><ol><li><strong>GitHub Actions OIDC</strong>: Use OIDC tokens instead of long-lived credentials for cloud access</li><li><strong>GitHub-hosted runners</strong>: Use GitHub-hosted runners for CI/CD to leverage ephemeral, secure build environments</li><li><strong>Environments</strong>: Use GitHub Actions <a href=https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/actions/how-tos/deploy/configure-and-manage-deployments/manage-environments target=_blank rel=noopener>environments</a> with protection rules for deployment stages</li><li><strong>Repository visibility</strong>: Default new repositories to private visibility</li></ol><p><strong>Environment protection rules</strong>:</p><div class="hextra-code-block hx-relative hx-mt-6 first:hx-mt-0 hx-group/code"><div><div class=highlight><pre tabindex=0 class=chroma><code class=language-yaml data-lang=yaml><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=c># Configure production environment protection</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w></span><span class=nt>Environment</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>production</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w></span><span class=nt>Protection rules</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w></span>- <span class=nt>Required reviewers</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>release-managers team</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w></span>- <span class=nt>Wait timer</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=m>30</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>minutes</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w></span>- <span class=nt>Deployment branches</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>Only protected branches (main, release/*)</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w></span>- <span class=nt>Environment secrets</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>Use scoped secrets with minimal permissions</span></span></span></code></pre></div></div><div class="hextra-code-copy-btn-container hx-opacity-0 hx-transition group-hover/code:hx-opacity-100 hx-flex hx-gap-1 hx-absolute hx-m-[11px] hx-right-0 hx-top-0"><button class="hextra-code-copy-btn hx-group/copybtn hx-transition-all active:hx-opacity-50 hx-bg-primary-700/5 hx-border hx-border-black/5 hx-text-gray-600 hover:hx-text-gray-900 hx-rounded-md hx-p-1.5 dark:hx-bg-primary-300/10 dark:hx-border-white/10 dark:hx-text-gray-400 dark:hover:hx-text-gray-50" title="Copy code"><div class="copy-icon group-[.copied]/copybtn:hx-hidden hx-pointer-events-none hx-h-4 hx-w-4"></div><div class="success-icon hx-hidden group-[.copied]/copybtn:hx-block hx-pointer-events-none hx-h-4 hx-w-4"></div></button></div></div><p>Leverage GitHub-hosted runners for most CI/CD workloads to benefit from automatic updates, security patches, and isolation. If self-hosted runners are necessary, follow these best practices:</p><ul><li>Run self-hosted runners on ephemeral VMs or containers</li><li>Use separate runner groups for different security zones</li><li>Restrict runner group access to specific repositories or organizations</li><li>Never use self-hosted runners for public repositories</li><li>Monitor audit logs for runner activity</li></ul><h3>Protect the Software (PS)<span class="hx-absolute -hx-mt-20" id=protect-the-software-ps></span>
<a href=#protect-the-software-ps class=subheading-anchor aria-label="Permalink for this section"></a></h3><h4>PS.1: Protect all forms of code from unauthorized access and tampering<span class="hx-absolute -hx-mt-20" id=ps1-protect-all-forms-of-code-from-unauthorized-access-and-tampering></span>
<a href=#ps1-protect-all-forms-of-code-from-unauthorized-access-and-tampering class=subheading-anchor aria-label="Permalink for this section"></a></h4><p><strong>Summary</strong>: Restrict access to source code, executables, and configuration-as-code based on the principle of least privilege.</p><p><strong>GitHub implementation scope</strong>: <em>GitHub Integration</em></p><p><strong>Implementation details</strong>:</p><p>Combine GitHub-native access controls with your identity provider.</p><ol><li><p><strong>Authentication</strong>: Require SAML SSO or OIDC for access to your GitHub environment</p><ul><li>Start by integrating your identity provider (IdP) with GitHub using either <a href=https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/admin/managing-iam/using-saml-for-enterprise-iam/configuring-saml-single-sign-on-for-your-enterprise target=_blank rel=noopener>SAML SSO</a> or <a href=https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/admin/managing-iam/configuring-authentication-for-enterprise-managed-users/configuring-oidc-for-enterprise-managed-users target=_blank rel=noopener>OIDC SSO</a>.</li><li>Prefer GitHub Enterprise Cloud with <a href=https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/admin/concepts/identity-and-access-management/enterprise-managed-users target=_blank rel=noopener>Enterprise Managed Users</a> (EMU) when you need centralized lifecycle management (provisioning, deprovisioning, and stronger governance boundaries) enforced by the IdP.</li><li>Turn on <a href=https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/admin/enforcing-policies/enforcing-policies-for-your-enterprise/enforcing-policies-for-security-settings-in-your-enterprise#requiring-two-factor-authentication-for-organizations-in-your-enterprise target=_blank rel=noopener>required 2FA for the enterprise</a> (or enforce MFA at the IdP if you use EMU).</li><li>Decide whether to allow a grace period for enrollment. Keep it short, communicate early, and remove access for users who do not comply.</li><li>Use audit logs to monitor MFA and sign-in related events and to verify that enforcement is working as intended.</li></ul></li><li><p><strong>IP allow lists</strong>: Restrict access to GitHub from approved IP ranges</p><ul><li>Configure an enterprise <a href=https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/admin/configuring-settings/hardening-security-for-your-enterprise/restricting-network-traffic-to-your-enterprise-with-an-ip-allow-list target=_blank rel=noopener>IP allow list</a> for web and API access to reduce exposure from untrusted networks.</li><li>Maintain a change-controlled process for adding and removing IP ranges (including temporary allowances for incident response).</li><li>If you plan to restrict CI/CD traffic by IP, validate compatibility with your runner model (e.g. GitHub-hosted runners).</li></ul></li><li><p><strong>Repository permissions</strong>: Use team-based access control with least privilege</p><ul><li>Set conservative organization defaults (for example, base permission set to no access) and grant access through teams instead of direct user-to-repo permissions.</li><li>Use role separation: keep organization owners limited, use repository admins sparingly, and grant broad visibility to security teams through the <a href=https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/organizations/managing-peoples-access-to-your-organization-with-roles/managing-security-managers-in-your-organization target=_blank rel=noopener>security manager</a> role.</li><li>Align access control design with your roles and responsibilities model in PO.2 to keep permissions understandable and auditable.</li></ul></li><li><p><strong>Repository rulesets</strong>: Prevent direct commits to protected branches</p><ul><li>Create organization-level rulesets that apply to all repositories (or to targeted repo sets using custom properties) and protect default branches and release branches.</li><li>Use rulesets to block high-risk operations such as force pushes and branch deletion, and to require pull requests for changes.</li><li>For deeper guidance on using rulesets as your policy engine (including monitoring bypass activity), see PO.4. For rulesets that enforce code scanning and dependency review, see PW.5.</li></ul></li><li><p><strong>Required reviews</strong>: Mandate code owner review for all changes</p><ul><li>Require pull requests before merge and configure minimum approvals, dismissal of stale approvals, and “require review from code owners”.</li><li>Maintain a CODEOWNERS file for security-sensitive paths (authentication, authorization, cryptography, payments, and the <code>.github</code> directory) to guarantee subject matter review.</li><li>For a complete example of review-focused ruleset configuration, see PW.7.</li></ul></li><li><p><strong>Push rulesets</strong>: Use push rulesets to further restrict changes to sensitive file paths</p><ul><li>Add a push ruleset to prevent or tightly control direct pushes to sensitive paths, such as <code>.github/workflows/**</code>, <code>CODEOWNERS</code>, IaC directories, and security policy files.</li><li>Use these rulesets to enforce “changes must flow through pull requests” for specific files even when other parts of the repository allow direct pushes.</li><li>Keep bypass permissions limited and review bypass events regularly.</li></ul></li><li><p><strong>Use commit signing</strong>: Configure commit signing using a GPG, SSH, or S/MIME key</p><ul><li>Standardize on a signing method that supports user interaction (passphrase prompts, biometrics, or hardware-backed keys) and document the supported options for developers.</li><li>Provide an onboarding path for developers to <a href=https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/authentication/managing-commit-signature-verification/about-commit-signature-verification target=_blank rel=noopener>configure signing keys</a> and validate verification status in pull requests.</li></ul><div class="hx-overflow-x-auto hx-mt-6 hx-flex hx-rounded-lg hx-border hx-py-2 ltr:hx-pr-4 rtl:hx-pl-4 contrast-more:hx-border-current contrast-more:dark:hx-border-current hx-border-blue-200 hx-bg-blue-100 hx-text-blue-900 dark:hx-border-blue-200/30 dark:hx-bg-blue-900/30 dark:hx-text-blue-200"><div class="ltr:hx-pl-3 ltr:hx-pr-2 rtl:hx-pr-3 rtl:hx-pl-2"><div class="hx-select-none hx-text-xl" style="font-family:apple color emoji,segoe ui emoji,segoe ui symbol">ℹ️</div></div><div class="hx-w-full hx-min-w-0 hx-leading-7"><div class="hx-mt-6 hx-leading-7 first:hx-mt-0"><strong>Why user interaction matters</strong>: The key defense here isn’t just the cryptographic signature - it’s the human verification step. A malicious script running on your machine can access your signing key, but it can’t press your fingerprint to the sensor or type your passphrase. This human-in-the-loop requirement is what blocks automated attacks from creating commits on your behalf.</div></div></div></li></ol><h4>PS.2: Provide a mechanism for verifying software release integrity<span class="hx-absolute -hx-mt-20" id=ps2-provide-a-mechanism-for-verifying-software-release-integrity></span>
<a href=#ps2-provide-a-mechanism-for-verifying-software-release-integrity class=subheading-anchor aria-label="Permalink for this section"></a></h4><p><strong>Summary</strong>: Enable software acquirers to verify that releases are legitimate and untampered.</p><p><strong>GitHub implementation scope</strong>: <em>GitHub Integration</em></p><p><strong>Implementation details</strong>:</p><p>Use artifact attestations to enable build provenance verification.</p><ol><li><strong>Artifact attestations</strong>: Generate cryptographic attestations for build artifacts using GitHub&rsquo;s <a href=https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/actions/how-tos/secure-your-work/use-artifact-attestations/use-artifact-attestations target=_blank rel=noopener>artifact attestation</a> feature</li><li><strong>Immutable releases</strong>: Leverage GitHub&rsquo;s <a href=https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/code-security/supply-chain-security/understanding-your-software-supply-chain/immutable-releases target=_blank rel=noopener>immutable releases</a> feature to prevent post-release modifications</li></ol><p><strong>Artifact attestation workflow</strong>:</p><div class="hextra-code-block hx-relative hx-mt-6 first:hx-mt-0 hx-group/code"><div><div class=highlight><pre tabindex=0 class=chroma><code class=language-yaml data-lang=yaml><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=nt>name</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>Build and Attest Container Image</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w></span><span class=nt>on</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>  </span><span class=nt>push</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>    </span><span class=nt>tags</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span>- <span class=s1>&#39;v*&#39;</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w></span><span class=nt>permissions</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>  </span><span class=nt>contents</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>read</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>  </span><span class=nt>id-token</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>write</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>  </span><span class=nt>attestations</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>write</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w></span><span class=nt>jobs</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>  </span><span class=nt>build-and-attest</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>    </span><span class=nt>runs-on</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>ubuntu-latest</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>    </span><span class=nt>steps</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span>- <span class=nt>uses</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>actions/checkout@v5</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span>- <span class=nt>name</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>Set up Docker Buildx</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>uses</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>docker/setup-buildx-action@v3</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span>- <span class=nt>name</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>Login to Docker Hub</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>uses</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>docker/login-action@v3</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>with</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>          </span><span class=nt>username</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>${{ secrets.DOCKERHUB_USERNAME }}</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>          </span><span class=nt>password</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>${{ secrets.DOCKERHUB_TOKEN }}</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span>- <span class=nt>name</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>Extract metadata</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>id</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>meta</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>uses</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>docker/metadata-action@v5</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>with</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>          </span><span class=nt>images</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>myorg/myapp</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>          </span><span class=nt>tags</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=p>|</span><span class=sd>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=sd>            type=semver,pattern={{version}}
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=sd>            type=semver,pattern={{major}}.{{minor}}</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span>- <span class=nt>name</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>Build and push</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>id</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>push</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>uses</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>docker/build-push-action@v6</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>with</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>          </span><span class=nt>context</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>.</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>          </span><span class=nt>push</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=kc>true</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>          </span><span class=nt>tags</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>${{ steps.meta.outputs.tags }}</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>          </span><span class=nt>labels</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>${{ steps.meta.outputs.labels }}</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span>- <span class=nt>name</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>Generate artifact attestation</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>uses</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>actions/attest-build-provenance@v3</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>with</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>          </span><span class=nt>subject-name</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>myorg/myapp</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>          </span><span class=nt>subject-digest</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>${{ steps.push.outputs.digest }}</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>          </span><span class=nt>push-to-registry</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=kc>true</span></span></span></code></pre></div></div><div class="hextra-code-copy-btn-container hx-opacity-0 hx-transition group-hover/code:hx-opacity-100 hx-flex hx-gap-1 hx-absolute hx-m-[11px] hx-right-0 hx-top-0"><button class="hextra-code-copy-btn hx-group/copybtn hx-transition-all active:hx-opacity-50 hx-bg-primary-700/5 hx-border hx-border-black/5 hx-text-gray-600 hover:hx-text-gray-900 hx-rounded-md hx-p-1.5 dark:hx-bg-primary-300/10 dark:hx-border-white/10 dark:hx-text-gray-400 dark:hover:hx-text-gray-50" title="Copy code"><div class="copy-icon group-[.copied]/copybtn:hx-hidden hx-pointer-events-none hx-h-4 hx-w-4"></div><div class="success-icon hx-hidden group-[.copied]/copybtn:hx-block hx-pointer-events-none hx-h-4 hx-w-4"></div></button></div></div><p><strong>Verification for consumers</strong>:</p><div class="hextra-code-block hx-relative hx-mt-6 first:hx-mt-0 hx-group/code"><div><div class=highlight><pre tabindex=0 class=chroma><code class=language-bash data-lang=bash><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=c1># Verify container image attestation using the GitHub CLI</span>
</span></span><span class=line><span class=cl>gh attestation verify oci://myorg/myapp:v1.0.0 --owner my-org</span></span></code></pre></div></div><div class="hextra-code-copy-btn-container hx-opacity-0 hx-transition group-hover/code:hx-opacity-100 hx-flex hx-gap-1 hx-absolute hx-m-[11px] hx-right-0 hx-top-0"><button class="hextra-code-copy-btn hx-group/copybtn hx-transition-all active:hx-opacity-50 hx-bg-primary-700/5 hx-border hx-border-black/5 hx-text-gray-600 hover:hx-text-gray-900 hx-rounded-md hx-p-1.5 dark:hx-bg-primary-300/10 dark:hx-border-white/10 dark:hx-text-gray-400 dark:hover:hx-text-gray-50" title="Copy code"><div class="copy-icon group-[.copied]/copybtn:hx-hidden hx-pointer-events-none hx-h-4 hx-w-4"></div><div class="success-icon hx-hidden group-[.copied]/copybtn:hx-block hx-pointer-events-none hx-h-4 hx-w-4"></div></button></div></div><h4>PS.3: Archive and protect each software release<span class="hx-absolute -hx-mt-20" id=ps3-archive-and-protect-each-software-release></span>
<a href=#ps3-archive-and-protect-each-software-release class=subheading-anchor aria-label="Permalink for this section"></a></h4><p><strong>Summary</strong>: Preserve release artifacts and provenance data to support vulnerability analysis and incident response.</p><p><strong>GitHub implementation scope</strong>: <em>GitHub Integration</em></p><p><strong>Implementation details</strong>:</p><ol><li><strong>GitHub Releases</strong>: Create immutable releases for each version</li><li><strong>Release artifacts</strong>: Attach binaries, containers, and other artifacts</li><li><strong>Tag protection</strong>: Protect release tags from deletion or modification using <a href=https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/organizations/managing-organization-settings/creating-rulesets-for-repositories-in-your-organization#creating-a-branch-or-tag-ruleset target=_blank rel=noopener>tag rulesets</a></li><li><strong>SBOM generation</strong>: Generate and attach SBOMs to releases</li></ol><p><strong>SBOM generation workflow</strong>:</p><div class="hextra-code-block hx-relative hx-mt-6 first:hx-mt-0 hx-group/code"><div><div class=highlight><pre tabindex=0 class=chroma><code class=language-yaml data-lang=yaml><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=nt>name</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>Generate SBOM</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w></span><span class=nt>on</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>  </span><span class=nt>push</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>    </span><span class=nt>tags</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span>- <span class=s1>&#39;v*&#39;</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w></span><span class=nt>permissions</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>  </span><span class=nt>id-token</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>write</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>  </span><span class=nt>attestations</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>write</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>  </span><span class=nt>contents</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>read</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w></span><span class=nt>jobs</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>  </span><span class=nt>build-and-generate-sbom</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>    </span><span class=nt>runs-on</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>ubuntu-latest</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>    </span><span class=nt>steps</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span>- <span class=nt>uses</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>actions/checkout@v5</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span><span class=c># Build steps for your software would go here</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span>- <span class=nt>name</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>Generate SBOM</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>uses</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>anchore/sbom-action@v0</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>with</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>          </span><span class=nt>format</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>spdx-json</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>          </span><span class=nt>output-file</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>sbom.spdx.json</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span>- <span class=nt>name</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>Attest SBOM</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>uses</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>actions/attest-sbom@v3</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>with</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>          </span><span class=nt>subject-path</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=s1>&#39;${{ github.workspace }}&#39;</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>          </span><span class=nt>sbom-path</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=s1>&#39;sbom.spdx.json&#39;</span></span></span></code></pre></div></div><div class="hextra-code-copy-btn-container hx-opacity-0 hx-transition group-hover/code:hx-opacity-100 hx-flex hx-gap-1 hx-absolute hx-m-[11px] hx-right-0 hx-top-0"><button class="hextra-code-copy-btn hx-group/copybtn hx-transition-all active:hx-opacity-50 hx-bg-primary-700/5 hx-border hx-border-black/5 hx-text-gray-600 hover:hx-text-gray-900 hx-rounded-md hx-p-1.5 dark:hx-bg-primary-300/10 dark:hx-border-white/10 dark:hx-text-gray-400 dark:hover:hx-text-gray-50" title="Copy code"><div class="copy-icon group-[.copied]/copybtn:hx-hidden hx-pointer-events-none hx-h-4 hx-w-4"></div><div class="success-icon hx-hidden group-[.copied]/copybtn:hx-block hx-pointer-events-none hx-h-4 hx-w-4"></div></button></div></div><h3>Produce Well-Secured Software (PW)<span class="hx-absolute -hx-mt-20" id=produce-well-secured-software-pw></span>
<a href=#produce-well-secured-software-pw class=subheading-anchor aria-label="Permalink for this section"></a></h3><h4>PW.1: Design software to meet security requirements and mitigate security risks<span class="hx-absolute -hx-mt-20" id=pw1-design-software-to-meet-security-requirements-and-mitigate-security-risks></span>
<a href=#pw1-design-software-to-meet-security-requirements-and-mitigate-security-risks class=subheading-anchor aria-label="Permalink for this section"></a></h4><p><strong>Summary</strong>: Use risk modeling to identify security requirements and design decisions.</p><p><strong>GitHub implementation scope</strong>: <em>GitHub-native</em> for documentation and tracking; <em>Out of scope</em> for threat modeling tools and methodologies</p><p><strong>Implementation details</strong>:</p><p>Document threat models and security decisions in GitHub.</p><ol><li>Use GitHub Issues to track security requirements derived from threat modeling</li><li>Use GitHub Projects to manage security risk mitigation efforts</li><li>Link security design decisions to implementation pull requests</li></ol><div class="hx-overflow-x-auto hx-mt-6 hx-flex hx-rounded-lg hx-border hx-py-2 ltr:hx-pr-4 rtl:hx-pl-4 contrast-more:hx-border-current contrast-more:dark:hx-border-current hx-border-blue-200 hx-bg-blue-100 hx-text-blue-900 dark:hx-border-blue-200/30 dark:hx-bg-blue-900/30 dark:hx-text-blue-200"><div class="ltr:hx-pl-3 ltr:hx-pr-2 rtl:hx-pr-3 rtl:hx-pl-2"><div class="hx-select-none hx-text-xl" style="font-family:apple color emoji,segoe ui emoji,segoe ui symbol">ℹ️</div></div><div class="hx-w-full hx-min-w-0 hx-leading-7"><div class="hx-mt-6 hx-leading-7 first:hx-mt-0">Threat modeling tools and methodologies should be selected based on organizational preferences. Results can be documented in GitHub.</div></div></div><h4>PW.2: Review the software design to verify compliance with security requirements<span class="hx-absolute -hx-mt-20" id=pw2-review-the-software-design-to-verify-compliance-with-security-requirements></span>
<a href=#pw2-review-the-software-design-to-verify-compliance-with-security-requirements class=subheading-anchor aria-label="Permalink for this section"></a></h4><p><strong>Summary</strong>: Have qualified reviewers confirm that software design meets security requirements.</p><p><strong>GitHub implementation scope</strong>: <em>GitHub-native</em></p><p><strong>Implementation details</strong>: Use GitHub Projects to track security requirements and verify design compliance.</p><ol><li><strong>Link security requirements to design reviews</strong>: Reference security requirement issues (from PO.1)</li><li><strong>Security team project access</strong>: Grant security teams read/write access to project boards tracking security requirements</li><li><strong>Design compliance verification</strong>: Security team reviews project boards to ensure all in-scope requirements are addressed in the design</li><li><strong>Status tracking</strong>: Reviewers validate designs and comment their approval or required changes in the related issue</li></ol><h4>PW.3: Verify third-party software complies with security requirements<span class="hx-absolute -hx-mt-20" id=pw3-verify-third-party-software-complies-with-security-requirements></span>
<a href=#pw3-verify-third-party-software-complies-with-security-requirements class=subheading-anchor aria-label="Permalink for this section"></a></h4><div class="hx-overflow-x-auto hx-mt-6 hx-flex hx-rounded-lg hx-border hx-py-2 ltr:hx-pr-4 rtl:hx-pl-4 contrast-more:hx-border-current contrast-more:dark:hx-border-current hx-border-blue-200 hx-bg-blue-100 hx-text-blue-900 dark:hx-border-blue-200/30 dark:hx-bg-blue-900/30 dark:hx-text-blue-200"><div class="ltr:hx-pl-3 ltr:hx-pr-2 rtl:hx-pr-3 rtl:hx-pl-2"><div class="hx-select-none hx-text-xl" style="font-family:apple color emoji,segoe ui emoji,segoe ui symbol">ℹ️</div></div><div class="hx-w-full hx-min-w-0 hx-leading-7"><div class="hx-mt-6 hx-leading-7 first:hx-mt-0">PW.3 has been replaced by PO.1 and PW.4 in NIST SSDF v1.1</div></div></div><h4>PW.4: Reuse existing, well-secured software when feasible<span class="hx-absolute -hx-mt-20" id=pw4-reuse-existing-well-secured-software-when-feasible></span>
<a href=#pw4-reuse-existing-well-secured-software-when-feasible class=subheading-anchor aria-label="Permalink for this section"></a></h4><p><strong>Summary</strong>: Use vetted libraries and components instead of reimplementing functionality.</p><p><strong>GitHub implementation scope</strong>: <em>GitHub-native</em></p><p><strong>Implementation details</strong>:</p><p>Use GitHub policies and dependency management features to enforce secure software reuse.</p><ol><li><p><strong>GitHub Actions policies</strong>: Set <a href=https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/admin/enforcing-policies/enforcing-policies-for-your-enterprise/enforcing-policies-for-github-actions-in-your-enterprise#policies target=_blank rel=noopener>enterprise policies</a> to only allow actions created by GitHub, verified Marketplace creators, or those that have been explicitly approved. Navigate to Enterprise Policies > Actions > Policies > and configure:</p><ul><li><input disabled type=checkbox> Allow all actions and reusable workflows</li><li><input disabled type=checkbox> Allow enterprise actions and reusable workflows</li><li><input checked disabled type=checkbox> Allow enterprise, and select non-enterprise, actions and reusable workflows<ul><li><input checked disabled type=checkbox> Allow actions created by GitHub</li><li><input checked disabled type=checkbox> Allow actions by Marketplace verified creators</li><li><input checked disabled type=checkbox> Allow or block specified actions and reusable workflows<ul><li>actions/*</li><li>github/*</li><li>docker/*</li><li>[etc&mldr;]</li></ul></li></ul></li></ul></li><li><p><strong>OIDC for cloud access</strong>: Enforce authentication to cloud providers through <a href=https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/actions/concepts/security/openid-connect target=_blank rel=noopener>Actions OIDC</a>, restricting access with a <a href=https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/actions/how-tos/secure-your-work/security-harden-deployments/oidc-with-reusable-workflows target=_blank rel=noopener>trust condition</a> that includes the <code>job_workflow_ref</code> variable. This prevents repositories from configuring cloud access directly and ensures consistent security controls. Store the cloud provider secrets (client ID, tenant ID, subscription ID) as organization secrets so they can be inherited by the caller workflows. Example reusable workflow for Azure access:</p><div class="hextra-code-block hx-relative hx-mt-6 first:hx-mt-0 hx-group/code"><div><div class=highlight><pre tabindex=0 class=chroma><code class=language-yaml data-lang=yaml><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=c># .github/workflows/reusable-azure-deploy.yml in your .github repository</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w></span><span class=nt>name</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>Reusable Azure Deployment</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w></span><span class=nt>on</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>  </span><span class=nt>workflow_call</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>    </span><span class=nt>inputs</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span><span class=nt>environment</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>required</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=kc>true</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>type</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>string</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>description</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=s1>&#39;Target environment (dev, staging, prod)&#39;</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span><span class=nt>azure-region</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>required</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=kc>true</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>type</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>string</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>description</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=s1>&#39;Azure region for deployment&#39;</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>    </span><span class=nt>secrets</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span><span class=nt>azure-client-id</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>required</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=kc>true</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>description</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=s1>&#39;Azure client ID for OIDC authentication&#39;</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span><span class=nt>azure-tenant-id</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>required</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=kc>true</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>description</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=s1>&#39;Azure tenant ID for OIDC authentication&#39;</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span><span class=nt>azure-subscription-id</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>required</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=kc>true</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>description</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=s1>&#39;Azure subscription ID for deployment&#39;</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w></span><span class=nt>permissions</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>  </span><span class=nt>id-token</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>write</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>  </span><span class=nt>contents</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>read</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w></span><span class=nt>jobs</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>  </span><span class=nt>deploy</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>    </span><span class=nt>runs-on</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>ubuntu-latest</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>    </span><span class=nt>environment</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>${{ inputs.environment }}</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>    </span><span class=nt>steps</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span>- <span class=nt>uses</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>actions/checkout@v5</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span>- <span class=nt>name</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>Azure Login using OIDC</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>uses</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>azure/login@v2</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>with</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>          </span><span class=nt>client-id</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>${{ secrets.azure-client-id }}</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>          </span><span class=nt>tenant-id</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>${{ secrets.azure-tenant-id }}</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>          </span><span class=nt>subscription-id</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>${{ secrets.azure-subscription-id }}</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span><span class=c># Deployment steps would follow</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span>- <span class=nt>name</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>Deploy to Azure</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>        </span><span class=nt>run</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=p>|</span><span class=sd>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=sd>          echo &#34;Deploying to ${{ inputs.environment }} in ${{ inputs.azure-region }}&#34;
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=sd>          # Add your deployment commands here</span></span></span></code></pre></div></div><div class="hextra-code-copy-btn-container hx-opacity-0 hx-transition group-hover/code:hx-opacity-100 hx-flex hx-gap-1 hx-absolute hx-m-[11px] hx-right-0 hx-top-0"><button class="hextra-code-copy-btn hx-group/copybtn hx-transition-all active:hx-opacity-50 hx-bg-primary-700/5 hx-border hx-border-black/5 hx-text-gray-600 hover:hx-text-gray-900 hx-rounded-md hx-p-1.5 dark:hx-bg-primary-300/10 dark:hx-border-white/10 dark:hx-text-gray-400 dark:hover:hx-text-gray-50" title="Copy code"><div class="copy-icon group-[.copied]/copybtn:hx-hidden hx-pointer-events-none hx-h-4 hx-w-4"></div><div class="success-icon hx-hidden group-[.copied]/copybtn:hx-block hx-pointer-events-none hx-h-4 hx-w-4"></div></button></div></div><p>Consumer repositories call this workflow instead of configuring cloud access directly:</p><div class="hextra-code-block hx-relative hx-mt-6 first:hx-mt-0 hx-group/code"><div><div class=highlight><pre tabindex=0 class=chroma><code class=language-yaml data-lang=yaml><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=c># In a repository&#39;s .github/workflows/deploy.yml</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w></span><span class=nt>name</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>Deploy to Azure</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w></span><span class=nt>on</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>  </span><span class=nt>push</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>    </span><span class=nt>branches</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=p>[</span><span class=l>main]</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w></span><span class=nt>jobs</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>  </span><span class=nt>deploy</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>    </span><span class=nt>uses</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>my-org/.github/.github/workflows/reusable-azure-deploy.yml@main</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>    </span><span class=nt>with</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span><span class=nt>environment</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>production</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span><span class=nt>azure-region</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>eastus</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>    </span><span class=nt>secrets</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span><span class=nt>azure-client-id</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>${{ secrets.AZURE_CLIENT_ID }}</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span><span class=nt>azure-tenant-id</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>${{ secrets.AZURE_TENANT_ID }}</span><span class=w>
</span></span></span><span class=line><span class=cl><span class=w>      </span><span class=nt>azure-subscription-id</span><span class=p>:</span><span class=w> </span><span class=l>${{ secrets.AZURE_SUBSCRIPTION_ID }}</span></span></span></code></pre></div></div><div class="hextra-code-copy-btn-container hx-opacity-0 hx-transition group-hover/code:hx-opacity-100 hx-flex hx-gap-1 hx-absolute hx-m-[11px] hx-right-0 hx-top-0"><button class="hextra-code-copy-btn hx-group/copybtn hx-transition-all active:hx-opacity-50 hx-bg-primary-700/5 hx-border hx-border-black/5 hx-text-gray-600 hover:hx-text-gray-900 hx-rounded-md hx-p-1.5 dark:hx-bg-primary-300/10 dark:hx-border-white/10 dark:hx-text-gray-400 dark:hover:hx-text-gray-50" title="Copy code"><div class="copy-icon group-[.copied]/copybtn:hx-hidden hx-pointer-events-none hx-h-4 hx-w-4"></div><div class="success-icon hx-hidden group-[.copied]/copybtn:hx-block hx-pointer-events-none hx-h-4 hx-w-4"></div></button></div></div><p>Benefits of centralized OIDC workflows:</p><ul><li>No long-lived cloud credentials stored in GitHub secrets</li><li>Consistent security controls across all cloud deployments</li><li>Centralized audit trail of cloud access</li><li>Simplified credential rotation and revocation</li><li>Reduced blast radius if a repository is compromised</li></ul></li><li><p><strong>Dependabot alerts</strong>: Enable Dependabot <a href=https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/code-security/dependabot/dependabot-alerts/about-dependabot-alerts target=_blank rel=noopener>alerts</a> and automated <a href=https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/code-security/dependabot/dependabot-security-updates/about-dependabot-security-updates target=_blank rel=noopener>security updates</a> for known vulnerabilities in dependencies.</p></li><li><p><strong>Dependency review</strong>: Use the <a href=https://github.com/actions/dependency-review-action target=_blank rel=noopener>dependency review</a> action to block new vulnerable dependencies and open-source software with non-compliant licenses. Refer to the example workflow file in section PW.5.</p></li></ol><h4>PW.5: Create source code by adhering to secure coding practices<span class="hx-absolute -hx-mt-20" id=pw5-create-source-code-by-adhering-to-secure-coding-practices></span>
<a href=#pw5-create-source-code-by-adhering-to-secure-coding-practices class=subheading-anchor aria-label="Permalink for this section"></a></h4><p><strong>Summary</strong>: Follow secure coding standards to minimize vulnerabilities.</p><p><strong>GitHub implementation scope</strong>: <em>GitHub-native</em></p><p><strong>Implementation details</strong>:</p><p>Use security configurations to enable and enforce security scanning, then use repository rulesets to prevent vulnerable code from being merged.</p><ol><li><p><strong>Security configurations</strong>: Refer to section PO.3 for detailed guidance on using GitHub&rsquo;s <a href=https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/code-security/how-tos/secure-at-scale/configure-enterprise-security/establish-complete-coverage/creating-a-custom-security-configuration-for-your-enterprise target=_blank rel=noopener>security configurations</a> to enable and enforce code scanning, secret scanning, and Dependabot across all repositories.</p></li><li><p><strong>Repository rulesets for code scanning enforcement</strong>: Use <a href=https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/repositories/configuring-branches-and-merges-in-your-repository/managing-rulesets/about-rulesets target=_blank rel=noopener>repository rulesets</a> to require code scanning results before code is merged. Configure the severity threshold for code scanning results that will fail a status check and block PRs from merging. This prevents net-new vulnerabilities from being introduced unless an authorized user intentionally bypasses the requirement. All bypass activity is captured by GitHub&rsquo;s audit log and reviewable in the ruleset insights tab, providing visibility for emergency deployments.</p><p>Example repository ruleset configuration:</p><ul><li><input checked disabled type=checkbox> Require code scanning results<ul><li>Tool: <code>CodeQL</code></li><li>Security alerts: <code>High or higher</code></li><li>Alerts: <code>Error</code> (blocks merge)</li></ul></li></ul><p>This configuration ensures that pull requests introducing high or critical severity vulnerabilities cannot be merged without explicit bypass authorization, which is logged and auditable.</p></li><li><p><strong>Repository rulesets for dependency review</strong>: Enforce a required workflow that includes <a href=https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/code-security/supply-chain-security/understanding-your-software-supply-chain/about-dependency-review target=_blank rel=noopener>Dependency Review</a>, which catches and blocks PR merges when changes introduce net-new vulnerable dependencies or add dependencies with restricted licenses.
